Ownership contract
- Client-provided ownership fields remain forbidden by validator.
- Any user/actor/organization/space values must come from trusted server context only.
- Persist helper must require trusted context separately from ControlledActivityIntakePayload.
- If userColumn/actorColumn is absent or ambiguous, implementation must remain blocked.

Metadata/idempotency contract
- idempotency trace should be stored in metadata/details/json column first.
- Dedicated idempotency column should only be used if exactIdempotencyColumn is confirmed above.
- If exactIdempotencyColumn is empty, this is not a blocker for static persist helper; use metadata column.
- A future additive migration can add a dedicated idempotency key/index later.

Status contract
- payloadBuilder currently uses raw_intake_pending_semantic_processing.
- safeStatusDecision: USE_PAYLOAD_RAW_STATUS_VALUE_WITH_STATIC_HELPER_ONLY
- If DB enum/check evidence does not confirm this value, runtime DB write remains blocked until a later proof gate.
- Static persist helper may still build the insert payload only if the selected status column is present and implementation remains unexecuted.
